Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the biotech structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 766. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 845 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to biotech structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 766/845 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 17. QED.
